### Q1. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>8</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#955;</mi><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>8</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></msup></mrow><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math> (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is the constant of integration), then the value of  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Let, <math><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>9</mn></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>8</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q2. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

Let <math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math> such that <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> and <math><mi>C</mi></math> is the constant of integration, then the value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msqrt><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><msqrt><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q3. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The integral <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>∫</mo><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>e</mi><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi><mo>,</mo></math> (where, <math><mi>c</mi></math> is the constant of integration). Then, <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> can be

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup><mo>⋅</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></msup><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math> <mrow> <mfenced close="}" open="{"> <mrow> <mtext>As,</mtext><mtext> </mtext><mo>∫</mo><msup> <mi>e</mi> <mi>x</mi> </msup> <mfenced> <mrow> <mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup> <mi>f</mi> <mo>′</mo> </msup> <mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced></mrow> </mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><msup> <mi>e</mi> <mi>x</mi> </msup> <mo>⋅</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced> <mi>x</mi> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q4. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of the integral <math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>6</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi>x</mi></msqrt><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>  
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

</details>

### Q5. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></msqrt></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> is equal to (where, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is the constant of integration)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></msqrt></math> <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
Substituting, <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><mi>t</mi></msqrt><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mi>t</mi><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>t</mi><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></mrow><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><msup><mi>t</mi><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mn>3</mn></mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>2</mn><mn>7</mn></mfrac><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mn>7</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

</details>

### Q6. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

<math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi></math>is equal to (where <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is an arbitarary constant)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Put <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></math>

so question becomes

<math><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">‍</mi></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>

</details>

### Q7. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>h</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> then the integral <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>g</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mi>h</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> simplifies to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><mi>&#955;</mi><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math> (where, <math><mi>C</mi></math> is the constant of integration). The value of <math><mi>&#955;</mi></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0.50**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given integral, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Let, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>⇒</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Ι</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q8. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math> simplifies to (where, <math><mi>c</mi></math> is the integration constant)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi></math>
Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

</details>

### Q9. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is the constant of integration)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Let, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>

Substituting, <math><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#921;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

### Q10. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If the integral <math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mi>k</mi><mi>ln</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi>x</mi></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math> (where <math><mi>C</mi></math> is the integration constant) and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mn>0</mn></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>, then the value of <math><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **6**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Let <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>7</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>t</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>∴</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>f</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>6</mn></math>

</details>

### Q11. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The value of <math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to (where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>c</mi></math> is the constant of integration)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>x</mi></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><msqrt><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>×</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

If <math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">∫</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>B</mi><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi><mo>,</mo></math> where <math><mi>A</mi></math> and <math><mi>B</mi></math> are constants and <math><mi>C</mi></math> is the constant of integration, then <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **0**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mo stretchy="false">&#8747;</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math>
So <math><mi>A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>B</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="|" close="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math></p>

</details>

### Q13. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>J</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>, then <math><mn>3</mn><mi>J</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi></math> is equal to (where <math><mi>C</mi></math> is the constant of integration)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">ln</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math><mn>3</mn><mi>J</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
Let <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>t</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></math>
So, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>J</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#8747;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>d</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow><mi>t</mi></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mi>t</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>C</mi></math></p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"/>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mi>J</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mfenced open="|" close="|" separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi>c</mi></math>

</details>
